var bot_mode := false


# every quit goes through here: free what statics hold first, then quit (see ShipView.clear_static)
func quit_clean(code := 0) -> void:
	if battle != null and is_instance_valid(battle):
		battle.queue_free()
	if campaign != null and is_instance_valid(campaign):
		campaign.queue_free()
	await get_tree().process_frame
	await get_tree().process_frame
	load("res://render/ship_view.gd").clear_static()
	load("res://scenes/battle/fx_stub.gd").clear_static()
	# other streams' statics (G4 fx and world, G3 screens): a clear_static() there is called too when it exists
	for sp in ["res://fx/fx.gd", "res://world/world.gd", "res://render/look.gd", "res://ui/campaign/campaign_ui.gd", "res://portraits/portraits.gd"]:
		if ResourceLoader.exists(sp):
			var sc = load(sp)
			if sc != null and sc.has_method("clear_static"):
				sc.clear_static()
	get_tree().quit(code)


# bot runs: count every engine and script error per run (Godot 4.5+ Logger), since a script error never reaches the run's
# own result
class ErrCount extends Logger:
	var n := 0
	var first: Array = []
	var mutex := Mutex.new()

	func _log_error(function: String, file: String, line: int, code: String, rationale: String, editor_notify: bool, error_type: int, script_backtraces: Array) -> void:
		if error_type == Logger.ERROR_TYPE_WARNING:
			return
		mutex.lock()
		n += 1
		if first.size() < 5:
			first.append("%s (%s:%d) %s" % [rationale if rationale != "" else code, file.get_file(), line, function])
		mutex.unlock()

	func _log_message(message: String, error: bool) -> void:
		pass

	func take() -> Array:
		mutex.lock()
		var out := [n, first.duplicate()]
		n = 0
		first.clear()
		mutex.unlock()
		return out


var errlog: ErrCount
var music: Node              # godot/music/music_player.gd (the music helper's player), one for the whole game


func _ready() -> void:
	name = "Main"
	_args()
	if DisplayServer.get_name() != "headless":
		DisplayServer.window_set_title(load("res://ui/battle/brand.gd").TITLE)  # brand.gd: the one place to rename the game
	bot_mode = args.has("bot-runs")
	# a fresh profile starts on Standard enemy AI (onboarding fix 1, campaign3d.js:3); bot runs ignore the player's
	# settings file and play Veteran, as sim3d does, unless --ai says otherwise
	if bot_mode:
		settings["ai"] = str(args.get("ai", "veteran"))
	else:
		_load_settings()
	_apply_settings()
	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://music/music_player.gd") and not args.has("bot-runs"):
		music = load("res://music/music_player.gd").new()
		music.name = "Music"
		add_child(music)
		music.set_volume(float(settings.get("music_volume", 0.7)))
		music.set_on(bool(settings.get("music", true)))
	if args.has("quit-after"):
		# a hard stop for test jobs, on every screen (real time, whatever the clock speed)
		var lim := float(args["quit-after"])
		get_tree().create_timer(lim, true, false, true).timeout.connect(func() -> void:
			print("[main] quit-after %d s reached, quitting" % int(lim))
			quit_clean(3))
	if args.has("check-export"):
		_check_export.call_deferred()
	elif bot_mode:
		_bot_runs.call_deferred()
	elif args.has("battle") or args.has("scenario") or args.has("shots") or args.has("bot") or args.has("seed"):
		# straight into a battle (test jobs, screenshot jobs): the battle reads the same arguments
		quick_battle()
	else:
		show_title()


# --check-export (run on the exported build): no test trees in the package, and the real parts of the game are in it
# (not the fallbacks). Prints [export-check] OK or FAIL with the reasons; exit code 0 or 1.
func _check_export() -> void:
	var bad: Array = []
	for d in ["res://rules/test", "res://campaign/test", "res://fx/test", "res://music/test", "res://story/test", "res://portraits/test", "res://audio/build"]:
		if DirAccess.dir_exists_absolute(d):
			bad.append("test folder shipped: " + d)
	for f in ["res://campaign/campaign.gd", "res://ui/campaign/campaign_ui.gd", "res://fx/fx.gd", "res://world/world.gd", "res://story/story.gd",
			"res://music/music_player.gd", "res://rules/battle_api.gd", "res://scenes/battle/battle.tscn"]:
		if not ResourceLoader.exists(f):
			bad.append("missing: " + f)
	for f in ["res://rules/data/tables.json"]:
		if not FileAccess.file_exists(f):
			bad.append("missing: " + f)
	if not DirAccess.dir_exists_absolute("res://gen"):
		bad.append("missing: res://gen (the built assets)")
	if not FileAccess.file_exists("res://music/music.json"):
		bad.append("note: no music content yet (res://music/music.json)")
	var fails := bad.filter(func(x): return not str(x).begins_with("note:"))
	print("[export-check] %s %s" % ["OK" if fails.is_empty() else "FAIL", JSON.stringify(bad)])
	quit_clean(0 if fails.is_empty() else 1)


func _args() -> void:
	var a := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	var i := 0
	while i < a.size():
		var k := a[i]
		if k.begins_with("--"):
			k = k.substr(2)
			if i + 1 < a.size() and not a[i + 1].begins_with("--"):
				args[k] = a[i + 1]
				i += 1
			else:
				args[k] = true
		i += 1

func start(m: Node, o: Dictionary) -> void:
	main = m
	opts = o
	bot = bool(o.get("bot", false))
	policy = str(o.get("policy", "smart"))
	t0 = Time.get_ticks_msec() / 1000.0
	C = Campaign.new()
	var err: String = C.load_tables()
	if err != "":
		errors.append("campaign tables: " + err)
	C.battle_mode = "external"
	C.battle_setup = _setup_battle
	C.battle_requested.connect(_on_battle)
	# the campaign's random stream: the browser seeds it at random on every load (core.js RNG_S, nothing saved), so a new
	# or continued run gets fresh draws each launch; bot runs seed it from --seed so each run differs and repeats exactly
	_seed_campaign(int(o.get("seed", 1)) if bot else randi())
	if bot:
		rng.seed = int(o.get("seed", 1))
		C.persist_on = false
		C.META = C.defaultMeta()
	else:
		C.persist_on = true
		C.persist_load()
	# story hook (story stream, godot/story/README.md): admiral lines, flagship name, titles, career records. Attached before
	# the first screen and before the UI binds, so it sees every change first; bot runs keep the career in memory only.
	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://story/story.gd"):
		story = load("res://story/story.gd").new()
		story.name = "Story"
		add_child(story)
		story.attach(C, not bot)
	if not bot: C.initFinishedRuns()
	C.set_teaching_tips(not bot and not Teaching.is_off() and not main.args.has("no-tips"))
	C.titleScreen()
	if o.get("continue", false):
		C.act("continueRun")
	elif o.has("screen"):
		C.act(str(o["screen"]))
	else:
		C.act("newRunScreen")
	C.set_teaching_tips(not bot and not Teaching.is_off() and not main.args.has("no-tips"))
	if bot:
		_bot_loop.call_deferred()
		return
	if ResourceLoader.exists(UI_G3):
		ui = load(UI_G3).new()
	else:
		ui = Fallback.new()
	if "hide_on_battle" in ui:
		ui.hide_on_battle = true
	add_child(ui)
	ui.bind(C)
	C.changed.connect(_on_changed)
	_on_changed()


func _seed_campaign(s: int) -> void:
	if C.has_method("seed_rng"):
		C.seed_rng(s & 0xFFFFFFFF)
	else:
		load("res://rules/jsm.gd").rnd_seed(s & 0xFFFFFFFF)  # fallback until campaign.seed_rng() lands (G3)


static func has_save(path: String = Campaign.SAVE_PATH) -> bool:
	return Campaign.has_saved_run(path)


var _music_page := ""


func _on_changed() -> void:
	C.set_teaching_tips(not bot and not Teaching.is_off() and not main.args.has("no-tips"))
	# the map's music per sector (the music helper's player)
	var kind := str(C.page.get("kind", ""))
	if main.music != null and kind != _music_page:
		_music_page = kind
		if kind == "map" and C.RUN != null:
			main.music.play_map(int(C.RUN.get("sector", 1)))
		elif kind == "title":
			main.music.play_title()
	# back on the title page (the player left the run, or the run ended and they pressed Title): hand back to main's title
	if not bot and C.page.get("kind", "") == "title" and C.modal_d == null:
		run_over.emit({"result": "title"})

def picture_target_matches(body):
    patterns = (
        r'!?\[[^\]]*\]\(\s*(<[^>]+>|[^\s)]+)',
        r'(?m)^\s*\[[^\]]+\]:\s*(<[^>]+>|\S+)',
        r'(?i)(?:src|href)=[\"\']([^\"\']+)',
        r'(?i)((?:https?://|file:/|[A-Za-z]:[\\/]|\\\\)[^\s<>\"\')]+\.(?:png|jpe?g|webp|gif)\b)',
    )
    for pattern in patterns:
        for match in re.finditer(pattern, body):
            target = unquote(match[1].strip('<>')).replace('\\', '/')
            if Path(urlsplit(target).path).suffix.lower() in EXTENSIONS:
                yield match


def picture_targets(body):
    return {match[1] for match in picture_target_matches(body)}


def check_body(repo, body, staged=False, tracked=True):
    """Require linked pictures AND sidecars in the PR tree. Never follow PC links."""
    repo = Path(repo).resolve()
    errors = []
    # Links inside code fences still reach the published PR and must be checked.
    targets = picture_targets(body)
    for target in set(targets):
        target = unquote(target.strip('<>'))
        suffix = Path(urlsplit(target.replace('\\', '/')).path).suffix.lower()
        if suffix not in EXTENSIONS:
            continue
        if re.match(r'(?i)(?:[a-z]+:|[/\\])', target) or '\\' in target:
            errors.append('use a relative repo picture link, never a PC or web path: ' + target)
            continue
        name = target.split('#')[0].split('?')[0]
        try:
            path = (repo / name).resolve()
            path.relative_to(repo)
            verify(path)
            for file in (path, sidecar(path)):
                relative = file.relative_to(repo).as_posix()
                if staged:
                    indexed = git(repo, 'show', ':' + relative)
                    same = (json.loads(indexed) == json.loads(file.read_bytes())) if file == sidecar(path) else indexed == file.read_bytes()
                    if not same:
                        raise ValueError('picture and sidecar must match the staged PR tree: ' + relative)
                elif tracked:
                    git(repo, 'ls-files', '--error-unmatch', '--', relative)
        except (ValueError, OSError, KeyError, subprocess.CalledProcessError) as exc:
            errors.append('review picture refused: ' + name + ': ' + str(exc))
    if errors:
        raise ValueError('\n'.join(errors))
    return len(set(targets))


def publish_report(repo, out, destination):
    """Copy only the linked, validated shots. Rewrite the report before staging."""
    repo, out = Path(repo).resolve(), Path(out).resolve()
    report = out / 'last.md'
    if not report.exists():
        return
    body = report.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
    copies, replacements = [], []
    matches = sorted(picture_target_matches(body), key=lambda match: (match.start(1), -match.end(1)))
    previous_end = 0
    for match in matches:
        start, end = match.span(1)
        if start < previous_end:
            continue  # A complete link already covers this bare path match.
        previous_end = end
        if match[1].startswith('<') and match[1].endswith('>'):
            start, end = start + 1, end - 1
        old = body[start:end]
        path_text, *suffix = re.split(r'(?=[?#])', old, maxsplit=1)
        name = unquote(path_text)
        candidate = Path(name)
        absolute = candidate.is_absolute()
        if name.replace('\\', '/').startswith('//') or (not absolute and re.match(r'(?i)[a-z]+:', name)):
            continue  # Leave UNC paths and URLs to check_body.
        if not absolute:
            candidate = out / candidate
        try:
            resolved = candidate.resolve()
        except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError):
            continue
        try:
            relative = resolved.relative_to(out / 'shots')
        except ValueError:
            if not absolute:
                continue
            try:
                relative = resolved.relative_to(repo)
            except ValueError:
                continue
        else:
            verify(candidate)
            dest = repo / destination / relative
            dest.resolve().relative_to(repo)
            copies.append((candidate, dest))
            relative = dest.relative_to(repo)
        new = quote(relative.as_posix()) + ''.join(suffix)
        if old != new:
            replacements.append((start, end, old, new))
    for start, end, _, new in reversed(replacements):
        body = body[:start] + new + body[end:]
    for source, dest in copies:
        dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copyfile(source, dest)
        shutil.copyfile(sidecar(source), sidecar(dest))
    check_body(repo, body, tracked=False)
    report.write_text(body, encoding='utf-8')
    for _, _, old, new in replacements:
        print(f'Rewrote picture link: {old} -> {new}')


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command', required=True)
    stamp = sub.add_parser('stamp')
    stamp.add_argument('path')
    stamp.add_argument('--stage', required=True, choices=STAGES)
    stamp.add_argument('--repo', default=str(ROOT))
    stamp.add_argument('--new-render', action='store_true')
    check = sub.add_parser('check-body')
    check.add_argument('body')
    check.add_argument('--repo', default=str(ROOT))
    check.add_argument('--staged', action='store_true')
    publish = sub.add_parser('publish-report')
    publish.add_argument('out')
    publish.add_argument('--repo', default=str(ROOT))
    publish.add_argument('--destination', required=True)
    context = sub.add_parser('context')
    context.add_argument('--repo', default=str(ROOT))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        if args.command == 'context':
            print(json.dumps(revision(str(Path(args.repo).resolve()))))
            return 0
        if args.command == 'stamp':
            stamp_file(args.path, args.stage, args.repo, args.new_render)
        elif args.command == 'check-body':
            check_body(args.repo, Path(args.body).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'), args.staged)
        else:
            publish_report(args.repo, args.out, args.destination)
        print('PASS picture provenance ' + args.command)
    except Exception as exc:
        print('FAIL picture provenance: ' + str(exc), file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())

func _battle_cue(kind: String, description: String) -> void:
	if kind == "cut_start":
		last_cut = description
	elif kind == "cut_end":
		description = last_cut
		last_cut = ""
	_event(kind, {"text": description})


func _arrival(units: Array) -> void:
	for u in units:
		_event("arrival", {"ship": u.get("name", ""), "side": u.get("side", "")})


func _fleet() -> Array:
	var rows := []
	for u in battle.V.get("units", []):
		var sv = battle.views.get(u["id"])
		if sv == null or u.get("squadron", false) or u.get("dead", false):
			continue
		rows.append({"ship": u.get("name", ""), "class": u.get("cls", ""), "side": u.get("side", ""), "code": sv.code,
			"asset": ShipView.manifest().get("ships", {}).get(sv.code, {}).get("res", ""), "parted_hull": sv.a4})
	return rows


func _play_battle() -> void:
	var b := battle
	var start := _frame()
	while not b.ended and (b.api == null or b.warming or b.opening):
		if _frame() - start > 30 * FPS:
			_fail("opening did not finish")
			return
		await get_tree().process_frame
	_event("board", {"fleet": _fleet(), "voice_on": b.voice != null, "hud": b.hud.visible, "cut_ins": b.cine, "tips_on": not b.tips_off})
	if not headless and PlaceholderGuard.stop_if_any():
		_fail("a required installed hull is missing")
		return
	var deadline := _frame() + 600 * FPS
	while _frame() < deadline:
		if b.hud.endcard != null:
			_event("end_card", {"result": b.result.get("result"), "rounds": b.result.get("rounds"), "errors": b.result.get("errors"), "fleet": _fleet()})
			await _pause(3.0)
			_event("battle_end")
			if battle_number == 3:
				print("[player-path] completed three battles")
				report_file.close()
				main.quit_clean(0)
			elif b.result.get("result") != "win":
				_fail("the route lost Battle %d" % battle_number)
			else:
				battle = null
				_press(b.hud, "Continue")
			return
		if not danger_seen and b._danger_shown() and not b._danger.is_empty():
			danger_seen = true
			_event("danger", {"round": b.V["round"]})
		if b.hud.tipcard != null and not b.busy:
			_event("teaching_card", {"id": b.tip_current, "round": b.V["round"]})
			await _pause(3.0)
			_press(b.hud.tipcard, "Got it")
			await _pause(0.3)
		elif b._can_act() and not b.ended:
			if b.sel.is_empty():
				_event("next_ship", {"round": b.V["round"]})
				b._on_key(KEY_TAB)
				await _pause(0.4)
			else:
				await _orders(b)
		else:
			await get_tree().process_frame
	_fail("Battle %d exceeded its replay limit" % battle_number)


func _orders(b: Node) -> void:
	var u: Dictionary = b._sel_unit()
	var label := "%s:%s" % [b.V["round"], u["id"]]
	var foes: Array = b.V["units"].filter(func(e): return e["side"] == "enemy" and not e["dead"] and not e.get("squadron", false))
	var obj = b.V.get("obj")
	var rally: Dictionary = obj.get("hex", {}) if obj is Dictionary and obj.get("type") == "hold" else {}
	var plan := {}
	if not moved.has(label) and not foes.is_empty() and int(u.get("moveLeft", 0)) > 0:
		plan = _move_plan(b, u, foes, rally)
	if not rally.is_empty() and not plan.is_empty() and _distance(u, rally) > 1 and _distance(plan, rally) <= 1:
		moved[label] = true
		if await _move(b, u, plan):
			return
	var target: Dictionary = _target(b, foes, rally)
	if not target.is_empty():
		if b.api.can_command() and int(b.V["round"]) >= 2:
			_event("command", {"round": b.V["round"], "name": b._command_label()})
			b._on_key(KEY_C)
			await _pause(0.45)
			b._on_key(KEY_C)
			await _pause(0.2)
			return
		if b.target == target["id"]:
			b._on_key(KEY_ESCAPE)
		_point(b, target)
		await _pause(0.25)
		_click(b, target)
		if b.target == target["id"]:
			_event("target_preview", {"ship": target["name"], "round": b.V["round"]})
			await _pause(0.65)
			b._on_key(KEY_ENTER)
			await _pause(0.2)
			return
	if not plan.is_empty() and not moved.has(label):
		moved[label] = true
		if rally.is_empty() and (first_preview or int(b.V["round"]) > 1):
			if await _attack_move(b, foes):
				return
		if await _move(b, u, plan):
			return
	if b._ui(u["id"]).get("orders", {}).get("brace", false):
		b._on_key(KEY_B)
	else:
		# The same handler as the End ship button, without Space's real-time debounce.
		b._on_action("end", null)
	await _pause(0.2)


func _move(b: Node, u: Dictionary, plan: Dictionary) -> bool:
	_click(b, plan)
	if b.plan == null:
		return false
	_event("move_preview", {"hex": [plan.q, plan.r], "ship": u["name"], "round": b.V["round"]})
	await _pause(0.55)
	b._on_key(KEY_ENTER)
	await _pause(0.2)
	return true


func _attack_move(b: Node, foes: Array) -> bool:
	for e in foes:
		if b._attack_move(e["id"]) == null:
			continue
		_click(b, e)
		if b.atk != null:
			first_preview = true
			_event("attack_move_preview", {"ship": e["name"]})
			await _pause(0.85)
			b._on_key(KEY_ENTER)
			await _pause(0.2)
			return true
	return false


func _target(b: Node, foes: Array, rally: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var best := {}
	var rank := -INF
	for e in foes:
		var guns: Array = b._firing_set(e["id"])
		if guns.is_empty():
			continue
		var damage := 0.0
		for wi in guns:
			var odds: Dictionary = b._fire_preview(b.sel, wi, e["id"])
			damage += float(odds.get("hull", 0.0)) + 0.4 * float(odds.get("shield", 0.0))
		var score := damage + 6.0 * guns.size() - 0.12 * float(e["hp"])
		if not rally.is_empty() and _distance(e, rally) <= 1:
			score += 70.0
		if score > rank:
			best = e
			rank = score
	return best


func _move_plan(b: Node, u: Dictionary, foes: Array, rally: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var best := {}
	var rank := -INF
	for st in b.reach_map.values():
		if (int(st.q) == int(u.q) and int(st.r) == int(u.r)) or not b._unit_at(int(st.q), int(st.r)).is_empty():
			continue
		var nearest := INF
		for e in foes:
			nearest = minf(nearest, _distance(st, e))
		var score := float(st.get("guns", 0)) * 8.0 - nearest * 6.0 - float(st.cost) * 0.15 - 5.0 * int(b._danger_at(st))
		if not rally.is_empty():
			var d := _distance(st, rally)
			if _distance(u, rally) <= 1:
				continue
			score -= 45.0 * maxf(0.0, d - 1.0)
		if score > rank:
			best = st
			rank = score
	return best


static func _distance(a: Dictionary, b: Dictionary) -> float:
	var dq := float(a.q) - float(b.q)
	var dr := float(a.r) - float(b.r)
	return maxf(maxf(absf(dq), absf(dr)), absf(dq + dr))


func _point(b: Node, hex: Dictionary) -> void:
	var p: Vector2 = b.cam.unproject_position(b.board.hex_world(int(hex.q), int(hex.r)))
	# Drive the same board hover as a real mouse. The recorder never sends a command to the rules.
	b._on_hover(p)
	if not headless and get_viewport().get_visible_rect().has_point(p):
		get_viewport().warp_mouse(p)


func _click(b: Node, hex: Dictionary) -> void:
	_point(b, hex)
	b._on_click()
def open_picture(path, required=False):
    """Restore only the pixels hidden by the strip, for crops and measurements."""
    path = Path(path)
    record = verify(path) if sidecar(path).exists() or required else None
    with Image.open(path) as original:
        image = original.copy()
    if record:
        patch = Image.open(io.BytesIO(base64.b64decode(record['covered_pixels'][0])))
        image = image.convert(patch.mode)
        image.paste(patch, tuple(record['stamp_box'][:2]))
    return image


def save_picture(image, path, stage, sources=None, repo=None, **options):
    if stage not in STAGES:
        raise ValueError('unknown picture stage: ' + stage)
    path = Path(path)
    if path.suffix.lower() not in EXTENSIONS:
        raise ValueError('unsupported review image: ' + str(path))
    source_list = []
    for record in sources or []:
        for src in record['sources']:
            if src not in source_list:
                source_list.append(src)
    if not source_list:
        source_list = [dict(revision(str(Path(repo or ROOT).resolve())), stage=stage)]
    record = dict(source_list[0], schema=1, picture_name=path.name, stage=stage,
                  date=datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ'),
                  sources=source_list, inputs=[summary(s) for s in sources or []])
    strip = strip_for(record, image.size)
    box = [0, image.height - strip.height, strip.width, image.height]
    frames = [image] + list(options.pop('append_images', []))
    covered = []
    stamped = []
    for frame in frames:
        frame = frame.copy().convert('RGBA' if 'A' in frame.getbands() and path.suffix.lower() not in {'.jpg', '.jpeg'} else 'RGB')
        if frame.size != image.size:
            raise ValueError('animation frames must have the same size')
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        frame.crop(box).save(buf, format='PNG')
        covered.append(base64.b64encode(buf.getvalue()).decode('ascii'))
        frame.paste(strip, tuple(box[:2]))
        stamped.append(frame)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if len(stamped) > 1:
        options.update(save_all=True, append_images=stamped[1:])
    stamped[0].save(path, **options)
    with Image.open(path) as saved:
        strips = [digest(f.convert('RGB').crop(box).tobytes()) for f in ImageSequence.Iterator(saved)]
    record.update(stamp_box=box, strip_size=list(strip.size), strip_sha256=strips,
                  stamp_template_sha256=digest(strip.tobytes()),
                  covered_pixels=covered, image_sha256=digest(path.read_bytes()))
    sidecar(path).write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n')
    return record


def stamp_file(path, stage, repo=None, new_render=False):
    path = Path(path)
    if sidecar(path).exists() and not new_render:
        # Never turn a cached old capture into a new revision.
        return verify(path)
    with Image.open(path) as image:
        frames = [f.convert('RGBA') for f in ImageSequence.Iterator(image)]
        options = {}
        if image.format == 'GIF':
            options.update(duration=image.info.get('duration', 100), loop=image.info.get('loop', 0))
        elif image.format == 'JPEG':
            options.update(quality=95)
    return save_picture(frames[0], path, stage, repo=repo, append_images=frames[1:], **options)


class Review:
    """Collect inputs for one output at a time.

    take_sources reserves inputs alongside cached pixels. Pass those records to
    save(sources=...) whenever another output uses the same cached pictures.
    """
    def __init__(self, stage):
        self.stage = stage
        self.sources = []

    def open(self, path, reference=False):
        if not reference or sidecar(path).exists():
            record = verify(path)
            if record not in self.sources:
                self.sources.append(record)
        return open_picture(path)

    def take_sources(self):
        sources, self.sources = self.sources, []
        return sources

    def save(self, image, path, sources=(), **options):
        output_sources = []
        for source in [*sources, *self.sources]:
            if source not in output_sources:
                output_sources.append(source)
        if not output_sources:
            raise ValueError('review sheet needs stamped source pictures; rerun the capture, do not relabel an old picture')
        record = save_picture(image, path, self.stage, sources=output_sources, **options)
        self.sources.clear()
        return record

    def copy(self, source, destination):
        return self.save(self.open(source), destination)

+# Called before Main loads settings or opens any screen. Godot may already have

+static func prepare_profile() -> String:
+	var profile := OS.get_environment("VS_PLAYER_PATH_PROFILE").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var temp := OS.get_environment("TEMP").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var actual := ProjectSettings.globalize_path("user://").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var user_dir := str(ProjectSettings.get_setting("application/config/custom_user_dir_name"))
+	if not temp.is_absolute_path() or not profile.to_lower().begins_with(temp.to_lower() + "/voidsworn-player-path-") or \
+			actual.to_lower() != profile.path_join(user_dir).to_lower():
+		return "Godot must resolve user:// inside a dedicated temporary profile from player_path.bat"
+	if not DirAccess.get_files_at("user://").is_empty():
+		return "the recording profile already contains files"
+	var marker := FileAccess.open("user://player_path_started", FileAccess.WRITE)
+	if marker == null:
+		return "the recording profile is not writable"
+	marker.store_line("Player path recording profile")
+	marker.close()
+	return ""

'use strict';
// Node 18+, no dependencies. All child processes run from the repository root.
//
// Issue 358: the table tells the truth. A row has one of four words:
//   PASS  the check found zero failures inside the row
//   KNOWN only failures that sit on the baseline list, with the count
//   FAIL  anything else
//   SKIP  the check could not run on this machine (optional dependency)
// A PASS row never carries a failure count, a CHECK count or a differed count.
// A listed id that passes (or no longer runs) is an error until it is taken
// off the list. The pack CHECK count and the drift differed count are held
// against ceilings in checks_baseline.json that can only go down.
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../..');

// Issue 357: the rules math targets V8 in Node 22; another major gives a false parity failure.
if (require.main === module && process.versions.node.split('.')[0] !== '22') {
  console.error('FAIL: Node major 22 is required, found ' + process.versions.node + '. Use the bundled Node 22 at voidsworn_test/node/node.exe.');
  process.exit(1);
}

function readBaseline() {
  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'checks_baseline.json'), 'utf8'));
}

const ascii = s => String(s).replace(/[^\x20-\x7e\n\r\t]/g, '?');

// ---------- result-word rules (issue 358) ----------

function verdictLine(output) {
  const lines = String(output || '').split(/\r?\n/).map(l => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  for (let i = lines.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (/^(PASS|FAIL)\b/.test(lines[i])) return lines[i];
  return '';
}

function parseFindings(output) {
  const passIds = [], failIds = [];
  let summary = null;
  for (const raw of String(output || '').split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const line = raw.trim();
    let m = /^PASS\s+(\S+)(?:\s|$)/.exec(line);
    if (m) { passIds.push(m[1]); continue; }
    m = /^FAIL\s+(\S+)(?:\s|$)/.exec(line);
    if (m) { failIds.push(m[1]); continue; }
    m = /^(\d+)\s+tests,\s+(\d+)\s+pass,\s+(\d+)\s+fail/.exec(line);
    if (m) summary = { total: +m[1], pass: +m[2], fail: +m[3] };
  }
  return { passIds, failIds, summary };
}

function classifyFindings(run, baseline) {
  const { passIds, failIds, summary } = parseFindings(run.output);
  if (!summary) return { result: 'FAIL', detail: 'FAIL: missing findings summary' };
  if (passIds.length !== summary.pass || failIds.length !== summary.fail) {
    return { result: 'FAIL', detail: 'FAIL: parsed ' + passIds.length + ' pass / ' + failIds.length + ' fail lines, summary says ' + summary.pass + ' / ' + summary.fail };
  }
  const listed = new Set(baseline.knownFindingsFailures || []);
  const known = failIds.filter(id => listed.has(id));
  const unexpected = failIds.filter(id => !listed.has(id));
  const listedPasses = passIds.filter(id => listed.has(id));
  const seen = new Set([...passIds, ...failIds]);
  const listedMissing = [...listed].filter(id => !seen.has(id));
  if (listedPasses.length) return { result: 'FAIL', detail: 'FAIL: listed id(s) now pass, take them off the list: ' + listedPasses.join(', ') };
  if (unexpected.length) return { result: 'FAIL', detail: 'FAIL: ' + unexpected.length + ' new failure(s): ' + unexpected.join(', ') };
  if (listedMissing.length) return { result: 'FAIL', detail: 'FAIL: listed id(s) no longer run: ' + listedMissing.join(', ') };
  if (known.length) return { result: 'KNOWN', detail: 'KNOWN: ' + known.length + ' listed failure(s): ' + known.join(', ') };
  return { result: 'PASS', detail: 'PASS: all ' + summary.total + ' gated findings tests pass' };
}

function classifyPack(run, baseline) {
  const out = String(run.output || '');
  const m = /SUMMARY:\s*(\d+)\s+FAIL,\s*(\d+)\s+CHECK/.exec(out);
  if (!m) return { result: 'FAIL', detail: 'FAIL: missing check pack summary' };
  const fails = +m[1], checks = +m[2];
  const failLines = (out.match(/^FAIL\b/gm) || []).length;
  const ceiling = Number.isFinite(baseline.checksCeiling) ? baseline.checksCeiling : 0;
  if (run.code !== 0 || fails > 0 || failLines > 0) {
    return { result: 'FAIL', detail: 'FAIL: ' + fails + ' FAIL, ' + checks + ' CHECK' + (run.code !== 0 ? ' (exit ' + run.code + ')' : '') };
  }
  if (checks > ceiling) {
    return { result: 'FAIL', detail: 'FAIL: ' + checks + ' CHECK is above the baseline ceiling of ' + ceiling };
  }
  return { result: 'PASS', detail: 'PASS: within the pack baseline' };
}

function classifyDrift(run, baseline) {
  const out = String(run.output || '');
  let m = /DRIFT SUMMARY:[^\n]*?(\d+)\s+differed/.exec(out);
  if (!m) m = /constants:\s*\d+\s+entries compared,\s*\d+\s+matched,\s*(\d+)\s+differed/.exec(out);
  if (!m) return { result: 'FAIL', detail: 'FAIL: missing drift summary' };
  const differed = +m[1];
  const ceiling = Number.isFinite(baseline.driftDifferedCeiling) ? baseline.driftDifferedCeiling : 0;
  if (differed > ceiling) return { result: 'FAIL', detail: 'FAIL: ' + differed + ' differed is above the baseline ceiling of ' + ceiling };
  if (differed > 0) return { result: 'KNOWN', detail: 'KNOWN: ' + differed + ' constant difference(s) within the baseline' };
  return { result: 'PASS', detail: 'PASS: no constant drift' };
}

+def _verify_clip(path, record):
+    template = strip_for(record, (1280, 720))
+    crop = f'crop={template.width}:{template.height}:0:{720 - template.height}:exact=1'
+    duration = float(record['duration_s'])
+    if not 0 < duration <= 48:
+        raise ValueError('invalid review clip duration: ' + str(path))

+            '-vf', crop, '-frames:v', '1', '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24', '-threads', '1', '-'
+        ])
+        _check_strip(Image.frombytes('RGB', template.size, pixels), template, path)

+def file_sha256(path):
+    digest = hashlib.sha256()
+    with path.open('rb') as stream:
+        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
+            digest.update(chunk)
+    return digest.hexdigest()

 var headless := false
 
 
+# Called before Main loads settings or opens any screen. Godot may already have
+# created its own log and cache directories, so check the root's files.
+static func prepare_profile() -> String:
+	var profile := OS.get_environment("VS_PLAYER_PATH_PROFILE").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var temp := OS.get_environment("TEMP").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var actual := ProjectSettings.globalize_path("user://").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var user_dir := str(ProjectSettings.get_setting("application/config/custom_user_dir_name"))
+	if not temp.is_absolute_path() or not profile.to_lower().begins_with(temp.to_lower() + "/voidsworn-player-path-") or \
+			actual.to_lower() != profile.path_join(user_dir).to_lower():
+		return "Godot must resolve user:// inside a dedicated temporary profile from player_path.bat"
+	if not DirAccess.get_files_at("user://").is_empty():
+		return "the recording profile already contains files"
+	var marker := FileAccess.open("user://player_path_started", FileAccess.WRITE)
+	if marker == null:
+		return "the recording profile is not writable"
+	marker.store_line("Player path recording profile")
+	marker.close()
+	return ""

+def ffmpeg_executable():
+    command = os.environ.get('FFMPEG') or shutil.which('ffmpeg')
+    if command:
+        return command
+    import imageio_ffmpeg
+    return imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
+
+
+def _check_strip(strip, template, path):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    # JPEG, GIF and MP4 quantize pixels. The text still has to be present.
+    if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(strip, template)).mean) > 18:
+        raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the picture: ' + str(path))
+
+
+def _verify_clip(path, record):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    template = strip_for(record, (1280, 720))
+    letters = template.convert('L').point(lambda p: 255 if p > 180 else 0)
+    crop = f'crop={template.width}:{template.height}:0:{720 - template.height}:exact=1'
+    duration = float(record['duration_s'])
+    if not 0 < duration <= 48:
+        raise ValueError('invalid review clip duration: ' + str(path))
+    for offset in (0, duration / 2):
+        pixels = subprocess.check_output([
+            ffmpeg_executable(), '-loglevel', 'error', '-nostdin', '-ss', str(offset), '-i', str(path),
+            '-vf', crop, '-frames:v', '1', '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24', '-threads', '1', '-'
+        ])
+        actual = Image.frombytes('RGB', template.size, pixels)
+        if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(actual, template), letters).mean) > 45:
+            raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the clip: ' + str(path))
+
+

+    digest = hashlib.sha256()
+    with path.open('rb') as stream:

+            digest.update(chunk)
+    return digest.hexdigest()

 var headless := false
 
 
+# Called before Main loads settings or opens any screen. Godot may already have
+# created its own log and cache directories, so check the root's files.
+static func prepare_profile() -> String:
+	var profile := OS.get_environment("VS_PLAYER_PATH_PROFILE").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var temp := OS.get_environment("TEMP").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var actual := ProjectSettings.globalize_path("user://").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var user_dir := str(ProjectSettings.get_setting("application/config/custom_user_dir_name"))
+	if not temp.is_absolute_path() or not profile.to_lower().begins_with(temp.to_lower() + "/voidsworn-player-path-") or \
+			actual.to_lower() != profile.path_join(user_dir).to_lower():
+		return "Godot must resolve user:// inside a dedicated temporary profile from player_path.bat"
+	if not DirAccess.get_files_at("user://").is_empty():
+		return "the recording profile already contains files"
+	var marker := FileAccess.open("user://player_path_started", FileAccess.WRITE)
+	if marker == null:
+		return "the recording profile is not writable"
+	marker.store_line("Player path recording profile")
+	marker.close()
+	return ""
+

+def ffmpeg_executable():
+    command = os.environ.get('FFMPEG') or shutil.which('ffmpeg')
+    if command:
+        return command
+    import imageio_ffmpeg
+    return imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
+
+
+def _check_strip(strip, template, path):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    # JPEG, GIF and MP4 quantize pixels. The text still has to be present.
+    if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(strip, template)).mean) > 18:
+        raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the picture: ' + str(path))
+
+
+def _verify_clip(path, record):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    template = strip_for(record, (1280, 720))
+    letters = template.convert('L').point(lambda p: 255 if p > 180 else 0)
+    crop = f'crop={template.width}:{template.height}:0:{720 - template.height}:exact=1'
+    duration = float(record['duration_s'])
+    if not 0 < duration <= 48:
+        raise ValueError('invalid review clip duration: ' + str(path))
+    for offset in (0, duration / 2):
+        pixels = subprocess.check_output([
+            ffmpeg_executable(), '-loglevel', 'error', '-nostdin', '-ss', str(offset), '-i', str(path),
+            '-vf', crop, '-frames:v', '1', '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24', '-threads', '1', '-'
+        ])
+        actual = Image.frombytes('RGB', template.size, pixels)
+        if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(actual, template), letters).mean) > 45:
+            raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the clip: ' + str(path))
+
+

+def file_sha256(path):
+    digest = hashlib.sha256()
+    with path.open('rb') as stream:
+        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
+            digest.update(chunk)
+    return digest.hexdigest()

+# Called before Main loads settings or opens any screen. Godot may already have
+# created its own log and cache directories, so check the root's files.
+static func prepare_profile() -> String:
+	var profile := OS.get_environment("VS_PLAYER_PATH_PROFILE").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var temp := OS.get_environment("TEMP").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var actual := ProjectSettings.globalize_path("user://").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var user_dir := str(ProjectSettings.get_setting("application/config/custom_user_dir_name"))
+	if not temp.is_absolute_path() or not profile.to_lower().begins_with(temp.to_lower() + "/voidsworn-player-path-") or \
+			actual.to_lower() != profile.path_join(user_dir).to_lower():
+		return "Godot must resolve user:// inside a dedicated temporary profile from player_path.bat"
+	if not DirAccess.get_files_at("user://").is_empty():
+		return "the recording profile already contains files"
+	var marker := FileAccess.open("user://player_path_started", FileAccess.WRITE)
+	if marker == null:
+		return "the recording profile is not writable"
+	marker.store_line("Player path recording profile")
+	marker.close()
+	return ""

+def ffmpeg_executable():
+    command = os.environ.get('FFMPEG') or shutil.which('ffmpeg')
+    if command:
+        return command
+    import imageio_ffmpeg
+    return imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
+
+
+def _check_strip(strip, template, path):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    # JPEG, GIF and MP4 quantize pixels. The text still has to be present.
+    if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(strip, template)).mean) > 18:
+        raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the picture: ' + str(path))
+
+
+def _verify_clip(path, record):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    template = strip_for(record, (1280, 720))
+    if digest(template.tobytes()) != record['stamp_template_sha256']:
+        raise ValueError('the clip stamp differs from its provenance: ' + str(path))
+    letters = template.convert('L').point(lambda p: 255 if p > 180 else 0)
+    crop = f'crop={template.width}:{template.height}:0:{720 - template.height}:exact=1'
+    duration = float(record['duration_s'])
+    if not 0 < duration <= 48:
+        raise ValueError('invalid review clip duration: ' + str(path))
+    for offset in (0, duration / 2):
+        pixels = subprocess.check_output([
+            ffmpeg_executable(), '-loglevel', 'error', '-nostdin', '-ss', str(offset), '-i', str(path),
+            '-vf', crop, '-frames:v', '1', '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24', '-threads', '1', '-'
+        ])
+        actual = Image.frombytes('RGB', template.size, pixels)
+        if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(actual, template), letters).mean) > 45:
+            raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the clip: ' + str(path))
+
+

     record = json.loads(sidecar(path).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))

+def file_sha256(path):
+    digest = hashlib.sha256()
+    with path.open('rb') as stream:

+            digest.update(chunk)
+    return digest.hexdigest()

+# Called before Main loads settings or opens any screen. Godot may already have
+# created its own log and cache directories, so check the root's files.
+static func prepare_profile() -> String:
+	var profile := OS.get_environment("VS_PLAYER_PATH_PROFILE").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var temp := OS.get_environment("TEMP").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var actual := ProjectSettings.globalize_path("user://").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var user_dir := str(ProjectSettings.get_setting("application/config/custom_user_dir_name"))
+	if not temp.is_absolute_path() or not profile.to_lower().begins_with(temp.to_lower() + "/voidsworn-player-path-") or \
+			actual.to_lower() != profile.path_join(user_dir).to_lower():
+		return "Godot must resolve user:// inside a dedicated temporary profile from player_path.bat"
+	if not DirAccess.get_files_at("user://").is_empty():
+		return "the recording profile already contains files"
+	var marker := FileAccess.open("user://player_path_started", FileAccess.WRITE)
+	if marker == null:
+		return "the recording profile is not writable"
+	marker.store_line("Player path recording profile")
+	marker.close()
+	return ""

+def ffmpeg_executable():
+    command = os.environ.get('FFMPEG') or shutil.which('ffmpeg')
+    if command:
+        return command
+    import imageio_ffmpeg
+    return imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
+
+
+def _check_strip(strip, template, path):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    # JPEG, GIF and MP4 quantize pixels. The text still has to be present.
+    if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(strip, template)).mean) > 18:
+        raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the picture: ' + str(path))
+
+
+def _verify_clip(path, record):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    template = strip_for(record, (1280, 720))
+    if digest(template.tobytes()) != record['stamp_template_sha256']:
+        raise ValueError('the clip stamp differs from its provenance: ' + str(path))
+    letters = template.convert('L').point(lambda p: 255 if p > 180 else 0)
+    crop = f'crop={template.width}:{template.height}:0:{720 - template.height}:exact=1'
+    duration = float(record['duration_s'])
+    if not 0 < duration <= 48:
+        raise ValueError('invalid review clip duration: ' + str(path))
+    for offset in (0, duration / 2):
+        pixels = subprocess.check_output([
+            ffmpeg_executable(), '-loglevel', 'error', '-nostdin', '-ss', str(offset), '-i', str(path),
+            '-vf', crop, '-frames:v', '1', '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24', '-threads', '1', '-'
+        ])
+        actual = Image.frombytes('RGB', template.size, pixels)
+        if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(actual, template), letters).mean) > 45:
+            raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the clip: ' + str(path))
+

 var headless := false
 
 
+# Called before Main loads settings or opens any screen. Godot may already have
+# created its own log and cache directories, so check the root's files.
+static func prepare_profile() -> String:
+	var profile := OS.get_environment("VS_PLAYER_PATH_PROFILE").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var temp := OS.get_environment("TEMP").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var actual := ProjectSettings.globalize_path("user://").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var user_dir := str(ProjectSettings.get_setting("application/config/custom_user_dir_name"))
+	if not temp.is_absolute_path() or not profile.to_lower().begins_with(temp.to_lower() + "/voidsworn-player-path-") or \
+			actual.to_lower() != profile.path_join(user_dir).to_lower():
+		return "Godot must resolve user:// inside a dedicated temporary profile from player_path.bat"
+	if not DirAccess.get_files_at("user://").is_empty():
+		return "the recording profile already contains files"
+	var marker := FileAccess.open("user://player_path_started", FileAccess.WRITE)
+	if marker == null:
+		return "the recording profile is not writable"
+	marker.store_line("Player path recording profile")
+	marker.close()
+	return ""
+

+def ffmpeg_executable():
+    command = os.environ.get('FFMPEG') or shutil.which('ffmpeg')
+    if command:
+        return command
+    import imageio_ffmpeg
+    return imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
+
+
+def _check_strip(strip, template, path):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    # JPEG, GIF and MP4 quantize pixels. The text still has to be present.
+    if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(strip, template)).mean) > 18:
+        raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the picture: ' + str(path))
+
+
+def _verify_clip(path, record):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    template = strip_for(record, (1280, 720))
+    if digest(template.tobytes()) != record['stamp_template_sha256']:
+        raise ValueError('the clip stamp differs from its provenance: ' + str(path))
+    letters = template.convert('L').point(lambda p: 255 if p > 180 else 0)
+    crop = f'crop={template.width}:{template.height}:0:{720 - template.height}:exact=1'
+    duration = float(record['duration_s'])
+    if not 0 < duration < float('inf'):
+        raise ValueError('invalid review clip duration: ' + str(path))
+    for offset in (0, duration / 2):
+        pixels = subprocess.check_output([
+            ffmpeg_executable(), '-loglevel', 'error', '-nostdin', '-ss', str(offset), '-i', str(path),
+            '-vf', crop, '-frames:v', '1', '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24', '-threads', '1', '-'
+        ])
+        actual = Image.frombytes('RGB', template.size, pixels)
+        if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(actual, template), letters).mean) > 45:
+            raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the clip: ' + str(path))
+
+

+def file_sha256(path):
+    digest = hashlib.sha256()
+    with path.open('rb') as stream:
+        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
+            digest.update(chunk)
+    return digest.hexdigest()

+# Called before Main loads settings or opens any screen. Godot may already have
+# created its own log and cache directories, so check the root's files.
+static func prepare_profile() -> String:
+	var profile := OS.get_environment("VS_PLAYER_PATH_PROFILE").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var temp := OS.get_environment("TEMP").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var actual := ProjectSettings.globalize_path("user://").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var user_dir := str(ProjectSettings.get_setting("application/config/custom_user_dir_name"))
+	if not temp.is_absolute_path() or not profile.to_lower().begins_with(temp.to_lower() + "/voidsworn-player-path-") or \
+			actual.to_lower() != profile.path_join(user_dir).to_lower():
+		return "Godot must resolve user:// inside a dedicated temporary profile from player_path.bat"
+	if not DirAccess.get_files_at("user://").is_empty():
+		return "the recording profile already contains files"
+	var marker := FileAccess.open("user://player_path_started", FileAccess.WRITE)
+	if marker == null:
+		return "the recording profile is not writable"
+	marker.store_line("Player path recording profile")
+	marker.close()
+	return ""

+def ffmpeg_executable():
+    command = os.environ.get('FFMPEG') or shutil.which('ffmpeg')
+    if command:
+        return command
+    import imageio_ffmpeg
+    return imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
+
+
+def _check_strip(strip, template, path):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    # JPEG, GIF and MP4 quantize pixels. The text still has to be present.
+    if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(strip, template)).mean) > 18:
+        raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the picture: ' + str(path))
+
+
+def _verify_clip(path, record):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    template = strip_for(record, (1280, 720))
+    if digest(template.tobytes()) != record['stamp_template_sha256']:
+        raise ValueError('the clip stamp differs from its provenance: ' + str(path))
+    letters = template.convert('L').point(lambda p: 255 if p > 180 else 0)
+    crop = f'crop={template.width}:{template.height}:0:{720 - template.height}:exact=1'
+    duration = float(record['duration_s'])
+    if not 0 < duration < float('inf'):
+        raise ValueError('invalid review clip duration: ' + str(path))
+    for offset in (0, duration / 2):
+        pixels = subprocess.check_output([
+            ffmpeg_executable(), '-loglevel', 'error', '-nostdin', '-ss', str(offset), '-i', str(path),
+            '-vf', crop, '-frames:v', '1', '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24', '-threads', '1', '-'
+        ])
+        actual = Image.frombytes('RGB', template.size, pixels)
+        if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(actual, template), letters).mean) > 45:
+            raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the clip: ' + str(path))
+
+

 var headless := false
 
 
+# Called before Main loads settings or opens any screen. Godot may already have
+# created its own log and cache directories, so check the root's files.
+static func prepare_profile() -> String:
+	var profile := OS.get_environment("VS_PLAYER_PATH_PROFILE").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var temp := OS.get_environment("TEMP").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var actual := ProjectSettings.globalize_path("user://").replace("\\", "/").simplify_path().trim_suffix("/")
+	var user_dir := str(ProjectSettings.get_setting("application/config/custom_user_dir_name"))
+	if not temp.is_absolute_path() or not profile.to_lower().begins_with(temp.to_lower() + "/voidsworn-player-path-") or \
+			actual.to_lower() != profile.path_join(user_dir).to_lower():
+		return "Godot must resolve user:// inside a dedicated temporary profile from player_path.bat"
+	if not DirAccess.get_files_at("user://").is_empty():
+		return "the recording profile already contains files"
+	var marker := FileAccess.open("user://player_path_started", FileAccess.WRITE)
+	if marker == null:
+		return "the recording profile is not writable"
+	marker.store_line("Player path recording profile")
+	marker.close()
+	return ""

+def ffmpeg_executable():
+    command = os.environ.get('FFMPEG') or shutil.which('ffmpeg')
+    if command:
+        return command
+    import imageio_ffmpeg
+    return imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
+
+
+def _check_strip(strip, template, path):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    # JPEG, GIF and MP4 quantize pixels. The text still has to be present.
+    if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(strip, template)).mean) > 18:
+        raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the picture: ' + str(path))
+
+
+def _verify_clip(path, record):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    template = strip_for(record, (1280, 720))
+    if digest(template.tobytes()) != record['stamp_template_sha256']:
+        raise ValueError('the clip stamp differs from its provenance: ' + str(path))
+    letters = template.convert('L').point(lambda p: 255 if p > 180 else 0)
+    crop = f'crop={template.width}:{template.height}:0:{720 - template.height}:exact=1'
+    duration = float(record['duration_s'])
+    if not 0 < duration < float('inf'):
+        raise ValueError('invalid review clip duration: ' + str(path))
+    for offset in (0, duration / 2):
+        pixels = subprocess.check_output([
+            ffmpeg_executable(), '-loglevel', 'error', '-nostdin', '-ss', str(offset), '-i', str(path),
+            '-vf', crop, '-frames:v', '1', '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24', '-threads', '1', '-'
+        ])
+        actual = Image.frombytes('RGB', template.size, pixels)
+        if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(actual, template), letters).mean) > 45:
+            raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the clip: ' + str(path))
+
+

+def file_sha256(path):
+    digest = hashlib.sha256()
+    with path.open('rb') as stream:
+        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
+            digest.update(chunk)
+    return digest.hexdigest()

+def ffmpeg_executable():
+    command = os.environ.get('FFMPEG') or shutil.which('ffmpeg')
+    if command:
+        return command
+    import imageio_ffmpeg
+    return imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()
+
+
+def _verify_clip(path, record):
+    from PIL import ImageChops, ImageStat
+    template = strip_for(record, (1280, 720))
+    if digest(template.tobytes()) != record['stamp_template_sha256']:
+        raise ValueError('the clip stamp differs from its provenance: ' + str(path))
+    letters = template.convert('L').point(lambda p: 255 if p > 180 else 0)
+    crop = f'crop={template.width}:{template.height}:0:{720 - template.height}:exact=1'
+    duration = float(record['duration_s'])
+    if not 0 < duration < float('inf'):
+        raise ValueError('invalid review clip duration: ' + str(path))
+    ffmpeg = ffmpeg_executable()
+    for offset in (0, duration / 2):
+        pixels = subprocess.check_output([
+            ffmpeg, '-loglevel', 'error', '-nostdin', '-ss', str(offset), '-i', str(path),
+            '-vf', crop, '-frames:v', '1', '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24', '-threads', '1', '-'
+        ])
+        actual = Image.frombytes('RGB', template.size, pixels)
+        if max(ImageStat.Stat(ImageChops.difference(actual, template), letters).mean) > 45:
+            raise ValueError('provenance text is absent from the clip: ' + str(path))
+

function filesOf(file) {
  if (/(?:^|\/)(?:test|oracle)(?:\/|$)/.test(file.replace(/\\/g, '/'))) return [];
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return [];
  if (fs.statSync(file).isFile()) return /\.(js|gd|json)$/.test(file) || path.basename(file) === 'head.html' ? [file] : [];
  return fs.readdirSync(file).sort().flatMap(name => filesOf(path.join(file, name)));
}
function scan(files, pats, allow = {}, root = ROOT) {
  const hits = [];
  const escaped = s => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
  // Explicit noun exceptions: never add the generic plural suffix to warp
  // phrases. In particular, "warps" and "warped" are legal verbs.
  const warpNouns = ['warp', 'the Warp', 'warp drive', 'drop out of warp'];
  const rules = pats.hard.map(pattern => ({ type: 'HARD', pattern, re: new RegExp(escaped(pattern), 'g') }))
    .concat(pats.word.map(pattern => ({ type: 'WORD', pattern, re: new RegExp('\\b' + escaped(pattern) + (warpNouns.includes(pattern) ? '' : 's?') + '\\b', 'gi') })));
  for (const target of files) {
    const file = path.relative(root, target).replace(/\\/g, '/');
    const source = fs.readFileSync(target, 'utf8');
    let strings;
    if (target.endsWith('.html')) {
      // Hide comments and non-visible script/style contents without moving offsets.
      const visible = source.replace(/<!--[\s\S]*?-->|<(script|style)\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/\1\s*>/gi, s => s.replace(/[^\n]/g, ' '));
      strings = [...visible.matchAll(/(?:^|>)([^<]+)/g)].map(m => ({ text: m[1], start: m.index + m[0].length - m[1].length, raw: m[1] }));
    } else strings = literals(source, path.extname(target).slice(1));
    for (const item of strings) for (const rule of rules) {
      if (rule.type === 'WORD' && (item.wordContext || (!target.endsWith('.html') && /^[a-z0-9_.-]+$/.test(item.text)))) continue;
      if (Object.entries(allow).some(([sub, list]) => file.includes(sub.replace(/\\/g, '/')) && list.includes(rule.pattern))) continue;
      rule.re.lastIndex = 0;
      for (const match of item.text.matchAll(rule.re)) {
        // Translate decoded offsets back to source offsets, including escapes.
        let offset = 0;
        while (offset < item.raw.length && unescapeText(item.raw.slice(0, offset)).length < match.index) offset++;
        const line = source.slice(0, item.start + offset).split('\n').length;
        const start = Math.max(0, Math.min(match.index - 30, item.text.length - 60));
        hits.push({ file, line, type: rule.type, pattern: rule.pattern, snippet: item.text.slice(start, start + 60).replace(/\s+/g, ' ') });
      }
    }
  }
  return hits.sort((a, b) => a.file.localeCompare(b.file) || a.line - b.line || a.pattern.localeCompare(b.pattern));
}
function main(args) {
  const pats = patterns();
  const allowPath = path.join(__dirname, 'naming_lint_allow.json');
  const allow = fs.existsSync(allowPath) ? JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(allowPath, 'utf8')) : {};
  const targets = args.filter(a => a !== '--json');
  const defaults = ['data.js', 'core.js', 'battle.js', 'campaign_meta.js', 'campaign_run.js', 'campaign3d.js', 'ui3d.js', 'main3d.js', 'head.html', 'godot'];
  const files = [...new Set((targets.length ? targets : defaults).flatMap(t => filesOf(path.resolve(ROOT, t))))];
  const hits = scan(files, pats, allow);
  for (const row of pats.unparsed) console.error(ascii('Unparsed rename row ' + row.line + ': ' + row.row));
  if (args.includes('--json')) console.log(JSON.stringify(hits, null, 2).replace(/[^\x00-\x7f]/g, c => '\\u' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).padStart(4, '0')));
